\section{Unconditional extremal fibers and the real-tilt interface}
\label{sec:extremal-ldp}

The affine coefficient model gives one description of the fiber spectrum.  For
extremal questions there is a second, particularly rigid description: the
fibers are consecutive values of the ordinary Fibonacci partition function on
two explicit intervals.  This removes the recurrence assumptions that are
sometimes imposed in experimental treatments of the largest fibers.

\subsection{An exact interval model}

\begin{theorem}[Fiber--partition interval identity]
\label{thm:fiber-partition-interval}
Let \(m\ge0\) and \(0\le r<F_{m+2}\) be integers.  Then
\[
 d_m^\#(r)=
 \begin{cases}
 R(F_{m+2}+r),&0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2,\\
 R(r),&F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}.
 \end{cases}
\]
An interval with lower endpoint larger than its upper endpoint is understood
to be empty.  In particular, this convention covers \(m=0,1\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The raw value \(N_m(\omega)\) is a sum of a subset of
\(F_2,F_3,\ldots,F_{m+1}\).  Since the single unit weight is \(F_2\), the
number of words with raw value \(n<F_{m+2}\) is exactly \(R(n)\).  By
\Cref{prop:single-overflow}, a word in the fiber over residue \(r\) has raw
value either \(r\) or \(F_{m+2}+r\).

Suppose first that \(0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2\).  Then
\[
 F_{m+2}+r\le F_{m+3}-2<F_{m+3}.
\]
Consequently every ordinary Fibonacci partition of \(F_{m+2}+r\) uses only
parts from \(F_2,\ldots,F_{m+2}\).  Those using \(F_{m+2}\) are in bijection,
by deletion of that part, with the \(R(r)\) partitions of \(r\).  Those not
using \(F_{m+2}\) are precisely the raw words of value \(F_{m+2}+r\).  The
fiber also contains the \(R(r)\) raw words of value \(r\).  Therefore the two
copies of \(R(r)\) cancel between this decomposition and the fiber sum, giving
\[
 d_m^\#(r)
 =R(r)+\bigl(R(F_{m+2}+r)-R(r)\bigr)
 =R(F_{m+2}+r).
\]

Now suppose that \(F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}\).  The largest possible raw value is
\[
 \sum_{j=1}^m F_{j+1}=F_{m+3}-2,
\]
whereas
\[
 F_{m+2}+r\ge F_{m+3}-1.
\]
Thus no word in this fiber can have the overflow value \(F_{m+2}+r\).  Every
partition of \(r<F_{m+2}\) uses only \(F_2,\ldots,F_{m+1}\), so the remaining
raw words are counted by \(R(r)\).  This proves the second case.
\end{proof}

For a compact statement of the maximizing residues, put
\[
 G_j:=F_{j+1}\qquad(j\ge0),
\]
so \(G_0=G_1=1\) and \(G_{j+1}=G_j+G_{j-1}\).
For \(k\ge5\), define
\[
 I_{2k+1}:=
 \begin{cases}
 G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1,&k\text{ even},\\
 G_kG_{k-2}+1,&k\text{ odd}.
 \end{cases}
\]
For \(k\ge7\), define
\[
 I_{2k}:=
\begin{cases}
 G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1,&k\text{ even},\\
 G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1,&k\text{ odd},
 \end{cases}
\]
\[
 J_{2k}:=
 \begin{cases}
 G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1,&k\text{ even},\\
 G_kG_{k-2}+1,&k\text{ odd}.
 \end{cases}
\]

\begin{conventionlemma}
Let \((G_j)_{j\ge0}\) denote the convention of
\cite{KocabovaMasakovaPelantova2005}, namely \(G_0=G_1=1\), with
representations using the parts \(G_1,G_2,\ldots\).  Then
\(G_j=F_{j+1}\) for every \(j\ge0\), and the source representation-count
function is exactly \(R\).  In particular, the source formulas
\[
 \max_{G_{2a+1}\le n<G_{2a+2}}R(n)=G_{a+1},\qquad
 \max_{G_{2a+2}\le n<G_{2a+3}}R(n)=2G_a
\]
become
\[
 \max_{F_{2a+2}\le n<F_{2a+3}}R(n)=F_{a+2},\qquad
 \max_{F_{2a+3}\le n<F_{2a+4}}R(n)=2F_{a+1}.
\]
The generic maximizing arguments in the source are
\[
 G_{2k+1}-1+I_{2k+1},\qquad G_{2k+2}-1-I_{2k+1}
\]
in odd source length \(2k+1\), and
\[
 \begin{split}
 G_{2k}-1+I_{2k},&\quad G_{2k+1}-1-I_{2k},\\
 G_{2k}-1+J_{2k},&\quad G_{2k+1}-1-J_{2k}
 \end{split}
\]
in even source length \(2k\).  The exceptional source arguments and their
translated fiber residues are
\[
 \begin{array}{c|c|c}
 \text{source length}&\text{partition arguments}&\text{fiber residues}\\ \hline
 9&63,71,79&8,16,24\\
 12&270,296,304,312,338&37,63,71,79,105.
 \end{array}
\]
Here the last column subtracts \(G_9=F_{10}=55\) or
\(G_{12}=F_{13}=233\), respectively.
\end{conventionlemma}

\begin{proof}
The identity \(G_j=F_{j+1}\) follows from the initial values and recurrence.
The admissible source parts \(G_1,G_2,\ldots\) are therefore exactly our
parts \(F_2,F_3,\ldots\), including a single unit part.  The maximum and
argument formulas are Theorems~4.7 and~5.3 of
\cite{KocabovaMasakovaPelantova2005}; direct substitution gives the displayed
formulas and endpoint subtractions.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact largest fibers]
\label{thm:unconditional-extremal-fibers}
Let \(D_m=\max_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)\).  Then
\[
 \begin{gathered}
 D_0=D_1=1,\qquad D_{2k}=F_{k+2}\quad(k\ge0),\\
 D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1}\quad(k\ge1).
 \end{gathered}
\]
No auxiliary recurrence hypothesis is assumed.

For every \(m\ge0\), a visible word \(x\in X_m\) is a maximizer if and only if
its residue \(r=V_m(x)\) belongs to the following set.  If \(m=2k\ge10\), the
set is
\[
 \mathcal M_{2k}
 =\{I_{2k+1}-1,\;G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\}.
\]
If \(m=2k-1\ge13\), the set is
\[
 \mathcal M_{2k-1}
 =\{I_{2k}-1,\;J_{2k}-1,\;
       G_{2k-1}-1-I_{2k},\;G_{2k-1}-1-J_{2k}\}.
\]
The remaining maximizing sets are
\[
\begin{array}{c|c|l}
m&D_m&\mathcal M_m\\ \hline
0&1&\{0\}\\
1&1&\{0,1\}\\
2&2&\{0\}\\
3&2&\{0,1,3\}\\
4&3&\{0,3\}\\
5&4&\{3\}\\
6&5&\{3,8\}\\
7&6&\{3,8,11,16\}\\
8&8&\{8,16,24\}\\
9&10&\{16,24,29,37\}\\
11&16&\{37,63,71,79,105\}.
\end{array}
\]
The windows \(m=10\) and \(m=12\) are covered by the generic even formula
for \(\mathcal M_{2k}\); they require no additional exceptional rows.
In particular, apart from the displayed initial exceptions, the degeneracy of
the maximum is exactly \(2\) in even windows and exactly \(4\) in odd windows.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Let
\[
 M_\ell:=\max_{F_{\ell+1}\le n<F_{\ell+2}}R(n).
\]
By the Fibonacci-convention lemma, Theorem~4.7 of
\cite{KocabovaMasakovaPelantova2005} gives
\[
 M_{2a+1}=F_{a+2}\quad(a\ge0),
 \qquad
 M_{2a+2}=2F_{a+1}\quad(a\ge1),
\]
with \(M_1=M_2=1\).
The same paper classifies every argument attaining these maxima
\cite[Thm.~5.3]{KocabovaMasakovaPelantova2005}.

By \Cref{thm:fiber-partition-interval}, the first block of the fiber list is
\[
 \bigl(R(n)\bigr)_{F_{m+2}\le n\le F_{m+3}-2}.
\]
Its maximum is \(M_{m+1}\).  The second block consists of values \(R(n)\) with
\(F_{m+1}-1\le n<F_{m+2}\).  For \(m\ge4\), its maximum is at most \(M_m\), and
the displayed formulas give \(M_m<M_{m+1}\): if \(m=2a\), then
\(2F_a<F_{a+2}\), while if \(m=2a+1\), then
\(F_{a+2}<2F_{a+1}\).  Hence \(D_m=M_{m+1}\) for \(m\ge4\).  The same equality
for \(m=0,1,2,3\), with the stated exceptional degeneracies, follows by direct
enumeration of the at most eight raw words.  Substitution of \(m=2k\) and
\(m=2k+1\) into the formulas for \(M_{m+1}\) proves the asserted closed forms.

It remains to translate the arguments of the maximum.  The
Fibonacci-convention lemma states that the two generic maximizing integers of
odd source length \(2k+1\) are
\[
 G_{2k+1}-1+I_{2k+1},
 \qquad
 G_{2k+2}-1-I_{2k+1}.
\]
Subtracting the left endpoint \(G_{2k+1}=F_{m+2}\), as prescribed by the first
case of \Cref{thm:fiber-partition-interval}, gives
\(I_{2k+1}-1\) and \(G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\).  Similarly, the four generic
maximizing integers of even length \(2k\) are obtained by using \(I_{2k}\) and
\(J_{2k}\) in the same two symmetric positions.  Subtraction of
\(G_{2k}=F_{m+2}\) gives the four residues displayed in the theorem.  The
exceptional-argument table in that lemma gives \(m=8\) and \(m=11\) after the
same endpoint subtraction.  Direct evaluation of the finitely many shorter
cases gives the remaining rows of the theorem.  Since \(V_m\) is bijective,
this residue list classifies the visible maximizing words themselves.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact two-step extremal recurrence]
\label{cor:extremal-fiber-recurrence}
For every integer \(m\ge6\),
\[
 D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4}.
\]
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
If \(m=2k\), then the three terms are \(F_{k+2},F_{k+1},F_k\), respectively.
If \(m=2k+1\), they are \(2F_{k+1},2F_k,2F_{k-1}\), respectively.  In both
cases the assertion is the Fibonacci recurrence.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Higher extremal ranks]
\label{rem:higher-extremal-ranks-open}
The interval identity is an unconditional reduction of every distinct
multiplicity level and its degeneracy to the corresponding level sets of
\(R(n)\) on the two displayed intervals.  The cited extremal theorem classifies
the first distinct level, but it does not classify the second or arbitrary
fixed \(k\)-th distinct level.  Consequently no closed formula for all top-
\(k\) levels is asserted here.  Establishing such formulas is a separate open
interface; the accompanying finite computations are not used as a substitute
for that proof.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Negative-temperature freezing and the residual LDP interface}

For real \(t\), define the real collision sum
\[
 S_t(m):=\sum_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)^t.
\]
This is well defined for all \(t\in\RR\), since every fiber is nonempty.  The
interval-completeness lemma following \Cref{thm:affine-transfer} proves this
nonemptiness before negative powers are introduced.  The positive integer
specializations agree with the moments in
\Cref{def:difference-spectrum}.

The following lemma records exactly the convention transfer and uniform bound
needed below.

\begin{layercountlemma}
For \(j\ge0\), set
\[
 \mathcal I_j=[F_{j+1}-1,F_{j+2}-1),\qquad
 N_j(k)=\card{\{n\in\mathcal I_j:R(n)=k\}}.
\]
Let \(\Psi(k)\) be Weinstein's number of generating orbits at level \(k\).
Then
\[
 N_0(1)=0,\qquad N_j(1)=1\quad(j\ge1),
\]
and, for every \(j\ge0\) and \(k\ge2\),
\[
 N_j(k)\le 2\Psi(k).
\]
For clarity, eventual equality is a separate statement: \(N_j(2)=2\) for
\(j\ge4\), while Weinstein's theorem gives
\(N_j(k)=2\Psi(k)\) for \(k>2\) and \(j\ge2k\).
\end{layercountlemma}

\begin{proof}
Weinstein uses \(f_0=f_1=1\)
\cite[Secs.~3 and~5]{Weinstein2016}.  Thus
\[
 f_j=F_{j+1}\qquad(j\ge0),
\]
and his half-open layer \([f_j-1,f_{j+1}-1)\) is exactly
\(\mathcal I_j\).  His partition function also equals \(R\), because its
admissible parts \(f_1,f_2,\ldots\) are our \(F_2,F_3,\ldots\).

For \(n\ge1\), write Weinstein's Zeckendorf index partition as
\[
 Z(n)=\langle z(n,1),\ldots,z(n,\lambda(n))\rangle
\]
and define its largest index by
\[
 L(n):=z(n,\lambda(n))=\max Z(n).
\]
Weinstein's Lemma~2.19, together with
\(R(f_i-1)=1\), gives the endpoint-sensitive equivalence
\begin{equation}
 R(n)>1\quad\Longrightarrow\quad
 \bigl(n\in[f_j-1,f_{j+1}-1)\iff L(n)=j\bigr).
 \label{eq:layer-largest-index}
\end{equation}
Indeed, after the left endpoint \(f_j-1\) and the right endpoint
\(f_{j+1}-1\), both of which have partition value \(1\), are removed,
Lemma~2.19 identifies the remaining integers in the layer with those whose
largest Zeckendorf index is \(j\).

At level \(1\), Weinstein's description
\(\{n:R(n)=1\}=\{f_i-1:i\ge1\}\), together with the half-open endpoint
convention, places exactly the left endpoint \(f_j-1\) in each
\(\mathcal I_j\) for \(j\ge1\); \(\mathcal I_0\) is empty.

At level \(2\), Weinstein gives \(\Psi(2)=1\) and the two families
\[
 f_i-1+f_{i+3},\qquad f_{i+2}-1+f_{i+4}\qquad(i\ge0).
\]
The first lies in \([f_{i+3}-1,f_{i+4}-1)\), since the distances from
the two endpoints are \(f_i\) and \(f_{i+1}\); the second lies in
\([f_{i+4}-1,f_{i+5}-1)\), with the analogous upper distance
\(f_{i+1}\).  Hence a layer contains at most one member of each family,
so \(N_j(2)\le2=2\Psi(2)\), with equality for \(j\ge4\).

Now let \(k>2\).  By Weinstein's Theorem~3.8, every solution of
\(R(n)=k\) has a unique representation
\begin{equation}
 [a]\tau^\varepsilon\sigma^\delta(g),
 \qquad
 g\in G(k),\quad a\ge0,\quad \varepsilon,\delta\in\{0,1\},
 \label{eq:weinstein-orbit-normal-form}
\end{equation}
where \(G(k)\) is the set of \(\Psi(k)\) generating solutions.  We derive the
largest-index behavior of all four branches from Weinstein's explicit
formulas~(17)--(19).

First consider a number \(n\) in Weinstein's sheet \(N_1\), and let
\(\theta(n)\) and \(Z'(n)\) denote the initial-block parameter and the
Zeckendorf tail used in those formulas.  Thus \(Z'(n)\) is a nonempty terminal
subpartition of \(Z(n)\), and in particular \(\max Z'(n)=L(n)\).  Formula~(18)
states that
\[
 [a](n)=f_{2\theta(n)-2+2a}-1+\lVert f_{Z'(n)+2a}\rVert.
\]
The definition of \(\theta(n)\) gives
\begin{equation}
 z(n,\theta(n))\ge2\theta(n).
 \label{eq:theta-tail-separation}
\end{equation}
To see this directly, if \(\theta(n)=1\), then the defining first index is at
least \(3\).  If \(\theta(n)>1\), the indices preceding
\(z(n,\theta(n))\) differ successively by \(2\), the first index is at least
\(1\), and the gap at \(\theta(n)\) is at least \(3\).  Hence
\(z(n,\theta(n))\ge1+2(\theta(n)-2)+3=2\theta(n)\).
The identity
\[
 f_{2q}-1=f_1+f_3+\cdots+f_{2q-1}\qquad(q\ge0),
\]
with the sum empty for \(q=0\), and
\eqref{eq:theta-tail-separation} show that the displayed sum is already the
Zeckendorf decomposition
\[
 Z([a]n)
 =\langle1,3,\ldots,2\theta(n)+2a-3\rangle
   \sqcup\bigl(Z'(n)+2a\bigr).
\]
The terminal subpartition contains the largest index.  Hence
\begin{equation}
 L([a]n)=L(n)+2a.
 \label{eq:L-a-shift}
\end{equation}

Formula~(19) gives
\[
 \tau(n)=
 \begin{cases}
 f_{2\theta(n)}-1+\lVert f_{Z'(n)+1}\rVert,
   &z(n,\theta(n))\ \text{odd},\\
 f_{2\theta(n)-4}-1+\lVert f_{Z'(n)-1}\rVert,
   &z(n,\theta(n))\ \text{even}.
 \end{cases}
\]
Expanding the first terms by the same identity and using
\eqref{eq:theta-tail-separation} shows that the shifted tail begins at least
two indices after the prefix and therefore supplies the largest index.
Consequently
\begin{equation}
 L(\tau n)=
 \begin{cases}
 L(n)+1,&z(n,\theta(n))\ \text{odd},\\
 L(n)-1,&z(n,\theta(n))\ \text{even},
 \end{cases}
 \label{eq:L-tau-shift}
\end{equation}
In either case,
\[
 L(\tau n)\equiv L(n)+1\pmod2.
\]
Because \(\tau n\in N_1\), applying \eqref{eq:L-a-shift} to \(\tau n\)
also gives the exact formula
\begin{equation}
 L([a]\tau n)=L(\tau n)+2a.
 \label{eq:L-a-tau-shift}
\end{equation}
Finally, the definition of Weinstein's involution \(\sigma\) adds \(1\) to
every Zeckendorf index on \(N_1\).  Therefore
\begin{equation}
 Z(\sigma n)=Z(n)+1,
 \qquad L(\sigma n)=L(n)+1
 \qquad(n\in N_1).
 \label{eq:L-sigma-shift}
\end{equation}

Fix \(g\in G(k)\) and a layer index \(j\).  By
\eqref{eq:L-a-shift} and \eqref{eq:L-a-tau-shift}, the largest indices in the
two \(N_1\)-branches are
\[
 L([a]g)=L(g)+2a,
 \qquad
 L([a]\tau g)=L(\tau g)+2a,
\]
and hence range over the two progressions
\[
 \{L(g)+2a:a\ge0\},
 \qquad
 \{L(\tau g)+2a:a\ge0\}.
\]
Each progression contains at most one occurrence of \(j\), and
\eqref{eq:L-tau-shift} gives them opposite parities.  Their union therefore
contains at most one occurrence of \(j\).  By \eqref{eq:L-sigma-shift}, the
two \(\sigma\)-branches meet layer \(j\) exactly when their corresponding
\(N_1\)-branches meet layer \(j-1\), so together they contribute at most one
additional point.  Thus the orbit of \(g\) contributes at most two points to
every layer, including the early layers.  The freeness of Weinstein's action
and the fundamental-domain property of \(G(k)\) make the generating orbits
disjoint.  Summing over them yields
\[
 N_j(k)\le2\lvert G(k)\rvert=2\Psi(k).
\]
The hypothesis \(j\ge2k\) in Weinstein's Theorem~5.1 is needed to prove that
both possible orbit points occur, and hence to prove eventual equality; it is
not used in this upper bound.

Finally, Weinstein often writes the auxiliary layer with both endpoints.
The shared endpoint has partition value \(1\), so this distinction is
irrelevant for \(k\ge2\); the level \(1\) endpoint was assigned directly
above.  This completes the treatment of endpoints and early layers.
\end{proof}

We now show that an all-real analyticity ansatz cannot hold.  The obstruction
is a proved frozen half-line for the Fibonacci partition spectrum.  Let
\(\sigma_0>2\) be the unique real solution of
\begin{equation}
 \frac{\zeta(\sigma_0-1)}{\zeta(\sigma_0)}=2.
 \label{eq:freezing-exponent}
\end{equation}
Numerically, \(\sigma_0=2.478750785733960\ldots\).
The number \(-\sigma_0\) is a Dirichlet-series freezing bound: the argument
below does not determine whether \(( -\infty,-\sigma_0]\) is the maximal
zero-pressure interval.

\begin{theorem}[Negative-temperature freezing]
\label{thm:negative-temperature-freezing}
For every real \(t\le-\sigma_0\), the pressure exists unconditionally and
\[
 P(t):=\lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_t(m)=0.
\]
Consequently there is no real-analytic function on \(\RR\) which agrees with
the finite-window pressure limits wherever those limits exist and also
satisfies \(P(0)=\log\varphi\).  In particular, no everywhere finite pressure
extension can be both real analytic and strictly convex on \(\RR\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For \(s>2\), put
\[
 A(s):=\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{\phi_{\rm E}(n)}{n^s}
 =\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}-1,
\]
where \(\phi_{\rm E}\) is Euler's totient function.  Weinstein's free-monoid
decomposition and Tonelli's theorem give the following equality in
\([0,+\infty]\):
\begin{equation}
 1+\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\Psi(k)}{k^s}
 =\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}A(s)^r.
 \label{eq:weinstein-level-dirichlet-series}
\end{equation}
The geometric series is finite exactly when \(A(s)<1\), equivalently when
\(\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s)<2\); in that region its value is
\((2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))^{-1}\).  The totient Dirichlet series also shows
that the ratio is strictly decreasing from \(+\infty\) to \(1\) on
\((2,\infty)\).  Hence \Cref{eq:freezing-exponent} has exactly one solution,
and \Cref{eq:weinstein-level-dirichlet-series} is finite precisely for
\(s>\sigma_0\).

By \Cref{thm:fiber-partition-interval}, the collision sum is exactly the sum
of \(R(n)^t\) over two adjacent Fibonacci layers:
\begin{equation}
 S_t(m)=\sum_{n=F_{m+1}-1}^{F_{m+3}-2}R(n)^t.
 \label{eq:real-collision-two-layers}
\end{equation}
If \(t<-\sigma_0\), the uniform layer-count lemma and
\Cref{eq:weinstein-level-dirichlet-series} give, uniformly in \(m\),
\[
 1\le S_t(m)
 \le 2+4\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\Psi(k)k^t<\infty.
\]
Thus \(m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\to0\).

At \(t=-\sigma_0\), fix \(\varepsilon>0\).  Only levels \(k\le D_m\)
occur, and for each such level
\[
 k^{-\sigma_0}\le D_m^\varepsilon k^{-(\sigma_0+\varepsilon)}.
\]
The same layer-count estimate therefore gives
\[
 S_{-\sigma_0}(m)
 \le 2+4D_m^{\varepsilon}
       \sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\Psi(k)}{k^{\sigma_0+\varepsilon}}.
\]
The series is finite, while
\(m^{-1}\log D_m\to\frac12\log\varphi\) by
\Cref{thm:unconditional-extremal-fibers}.  Therefore
\[
 0\le\limsup_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_{-\sigma_0}(m)
 \le\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\log\varphi.
\]
Letting \(\varepsilon\downarrow0\) proves the endpoint assertion.

Finally, an analytic function which vanishes on the open interval
\(( -\infty,-\sigma_0)\) vanishes identically, whereas
\[
 P(0)=\lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log|X_m|=\log\varphi.
\]
This proves the analytic and strict-convexity obstructions.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[No globally analytic compact-operator representation]
\label{prop:no-analytic-compact-representation}
There do not exist a complex Banach space \(\mathcal B\), a connected open
set \(U\subset\mathbb C\) containing \(\RR\), and an operator-norm analytic
map
\[
 z\longmapsto\mathcal L_z\in\mathcal K(\mathcal B)
 \qquad(z\in U)
\]
into the compact operators with the following properties.  For every real
\(t\), the operator \(\mathcal L_t\) has a positive algebraically simple
isolated eigenvalue \(r(t)\); for every \(t_0\in\RR\), this eigenvalue is
separated from the remainder of the spectrum by a positive gap uniformly for
\(t\) in some real neighborhood of \(t_0\); and the family represents the
finite-window sums in the exponential sense
\[
 \lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_t(m)=\log r(t)
 \qquad(t\in\RR).
\]
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
For each \(m\ge1\), the function
\[
 p_m(t):=\frac1m\log S_t(m)
\]
is convex on \(\RR\).  Indeed, all fiber multiplicities are positive by the
interval-completeness lemma, and H\"older's inequality gives the defining
convexity inequality for \(S_t(m)\).  The representation hypothesis says that
\(p_m(t)\) converges pointwise on \(\RR\) to
\[
 P(t):=\log r(t).
\]
The finite pointwise limit \(P\) is therefore convex.  A finite convex function
on the open interval \(\RR\) is continuous, so \(r(t)=e^{P(t)}\) is continuous
on \(\RR\).

Fix \(t_0\in\RR\).  Choose a positively oriented contour \(\Gamma\) enclosing
\(r(t_0)\) and no other point of the spectrum of \(\mathcal L_{t_0}\).  For
complex \(z\) sufficiently close to \(t_0\), the contour remains in the
resolvent set and the Riesz projection
\[
 \Pi(z)=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_\Gamma
  (\lambda I-\mathcal L_z)^{-1}\,d\lambda
\]
is analytic.  Its rank is locally constant and equals the algebraic
multiplicity of \(r(t_0)\), hence is one.  Analytic perturbation theory
\cite[Chap.~VII, Sec.~1]{Kato1995} therefore supplies a unique analytic
eigenvalue branch \(\mu(z)\) inside \(\Gamma\), with
\(\mu(t_0)=r(t_0)\).  The continuity of \(r(t)\) puts \(r(t)\) inside
\(\Gamma\) for all real \(t\) sufficiently close to \(t_0\); the local
spectral separation and rank-one Riesz projection then force
\(r(t)=\mu(t)\).  Thus \(r\), and hence \(t\mapsto\log r(t)\), is locally real
analytic at every \(t_0\).

The representation hypothesis makes this function equal to \(P(t)\).  By
\Cref{thm:negative-temperature-freezing}, it vanishes on
\(( -\infty,-\sigma_0)\), and therefore vanishes identically.  This
contradicts \(P(0)=\log\varphi\).
\end{proof}

The remaining issue is therefore not global analyticity but the construction
and regularity of the pressure outside the proved frozen half-line.

\begin{pressurehypothesis}
\label{hyp:residual-real-pressure}
We say that the residual real-pressure hypothesis holds if the following
assertions are true.
\begin{enumerate}
\item For every \(t> -\sigma_0\), the finite limit
\[
 P(t)=\lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_t(m)
\]
exists.
\item The function obtained by adjoining the proven value \(P(t)=0\) for
\(t\le-\sigma_0\) is differentiable on \(\RR\); its restriction to
\(( -\sigma_0,\infty)\) is real analytic and satisfies \(P''(t)>0\).
\end{enumerate}
\end{pressurehypothesis}

\begin{corollary}[Conditional real-tilt large deviation principle]
\label{thm:conditional-full-ldp}
Assume the residual real-pressure hypothesis stated above.  Let \(X^{(m)}\) be
uniformly distributed on \(X_m\), and set
\[
 Y_m:=\frac1m\log d_m(X^{(m)}).
\]
Then \((Y_m)\) satisfies a full large deviation principle on \(\RR\) with speed
\(m\) and good convex rate function
\[
 I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\RR}
 \{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}.
\]
Thus, for every Borel set \(A\subseteq\RR\),
\[
 -\inf_{\alpha\in A^\circ}I(\alpha)
 \le \liminf_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log\Pr(Y_m\in A),
\]
\[
 \limsup_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log\Pr(Y_m\in A)
 \le-\inf_{\alpha\in\overline A}I(\alpha).
\]
The lower endpoint is \(\alpha_-=0\), the upper endpoint is
\(\alpha_+=\frac12\log\varphi\), and \(I(0)=\log\varphi\).  The function
\(I\) is real analytic and strictly convex on \((0,\alpha_+)\).  More
precisely, for the unique \(t(\alpha)>-\sigma_0\) satisfying
\(P'(t(\alpha))=\alpha\),
\[
 I'(\alpha)=t(\alpha),
 \qquad
 I''(\alpha)=\frac1{P''(t(\alpha))}>0.
\]
Moreover, \(\lim_{t\to+\infty}P(t)/t=\frac12\log\varphi\).
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Let \(\nu_m\) be the uniform law on \(X_m\).  For every \(t\in\RR\),
\begin{align*}
 \frac1m\log\EE_{\nu_m}e^{mtY_m}
 &=\frac1m\log\left(\frac1{F_{m+2}}
       \sum_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)^t\right)\\
 &\longrightarrow P(t)-\log\varphi=:\Lambda(t).
\end{align*}
By hypothesis, \(\Lambda\) is finite and differentiable on all of \(\RR\).
Moreover, \(0\le Y_m\le m^{-1}\log D_m\), and
\Cref{thm:unconditional-extremal-fibers} gives
\[
 \frac1m\log D_m\longrightarrow\frac12\log\varphi.
\]
The laws of \(Y_m\) are therefore exponentially tight (indeed, they are
eventually supported in one fixed compact interval).  The
G\"artner--Ellis theorem
\cite{Gartner1977,Ellis1984,DemboZeitouni2010}, applied on the
full effective domain \(\RR\), yields the stated full LDP with good rate
\[
 \Lambda^*(\alpha)
 =\sup_t\{t\alpha-\Lambda(t)\}
 =\sup_t\{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}.
\]

On \(( -\sigma_0,\infty)\), the derivative \(P'\) is strictly increasing.
The differentiability at \(-\sigma_0\) and
\Cref{thm:negative-temperature-freezing} give
\(P'(-\sigma_0)=0\), so the open derivative range is
\((0,\alpha_+)\).  The analytic
inverse function theorem gives an analytic inverse \(t(\alpha)\) on the open
range \((0,\alpha_+)\).  The supremum defining \(I(\alpha)\) is attained
there uniquely at \(t(\alpha)\).  Differentiating
\[
 I(\alpha)=t(\alpha)\alpha-P(t(\alpha))+\log\varphi
\]
gives \(I'(\alpha)=t(\alpha)\) and
\(I''(\alpha)=1/P''(t(\alpha))>0\).

For \(\alpha=0\), every \(t\le-\sigma_0\) gives
\(t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi=\log\varphi\).  Since \(S_t(m)\ge1\), every
existing pressure is nonnegative, and hence \(I(0)=\log\varphi\).

It remains to identify the upper endpoint.  For every real \(t>0\),
\[
 D_m^t\le S_t(m)\le F_{m+2}D_m^t.
\]
Taking logarithms, dividing by \(m\), and passing to the limit gives
\[
 \frac{t}{2}\log\varphi
 \le P(t)\le
 \log\varphi+\frac{t}{2}\log\varphi.
\]
Division by \(t\) proves the asserted limit of \(P(t)/t\).  For a differentiable
convex function, convergence of \(P(t)/t\) to a finite limit forces the
increasing derivative \(P'(t)\) to converge to the same limit: for any fixed
\(0<a<1<b\), convexity bounds \(P'(t)\) between the secant slopes on
\([at,t]\) and \([t,bt]\), and both tend to \(\frac12\log\varphi\).
Thus \(\alpha_+=\frac12\log\varphi\).
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Residual real-pressure interface]
\label{rem:real-pressure-open-interface}
\Cref{thm:negative-temperature-freezing} excludes a globally analytic
pressure, and \Cref{prop:no-analytic-compact-representation} states the
operator obstruction precisely.  Sanna's theorem and the finite-window
transfer determine \(P(q)=\log\lambda_q\) at positive integer orders, but
those discrete values do not define \(P(t)\) at noninteger \(t\), and the
freezing theorem only treats \(t\le-\sigma_0\).  Existence for every
\(t\in(-\sigma_0,\infty)\), differentiability at the boundary
\(-\sigma_0\) of the proved region, and analytic strict convexity on the
residual interval remain open.  In particular, positivity of \(P(t)\) for all
\(t> -\sigma_0\) has not been established.
One possible approach would require an induced or countable-state real-tilt
operator with explicit recurrence and spectral-gap estimates; a compact
analytic family satisfying \Cref{prop:no-analytic-compact-representation} is
impossible
\cite{Ruelle2004,Sarig1999,Sarig2001}.  Consequently the full LDP in
\Cref{thm:conditional-full-ldp} remains conditional precisely on
\Cref{hyp:residual-real-pressure}, and no unconditional strictly convex full
LDP is asserted.
\end{remark}

\section{Fibonacci windows and integer moment transfer}
\label{sec:second-collision}

By \Cref{thm:partition-difference}, the finite-window fiber spectrum is already
encoded by the classical Fibonacci partition function.  The collision moments
are therefore naturally expressed as power sums of a Fibonacci-lag discrete
derivative.  The key point of this section is that those power sums are
trapped between two adjacent Fibonacci windows of the classical partition
power sums.

\subsection{Difference-power sums and Fibonacci windows}

\begin{definition}[Difference spectrum]
\label{def:difference-spectrum}
For \(0\le n<F_{m+2}\), define
\[
  a_m(n):=d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))
  =
  R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1}).
\]
For an integer \(q\ge 1\), define
\[
  S_q(m):=\sum_{x\in X_m} d_m(x)^q,
  \qquad
  T_q^\dagger(N):=\sum_{n=0}^{N-1} R^\dagger(n)^q.
\]
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Difference-power sums]
\label{thm:difference-power-sums}
For every pair of integers \(m\ge1\) and \(q\ge 1\),
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1} a_m(n)^q
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}
  \bigl(R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\bigr)^q.
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Because \(\pi_m\) is a permutation of \(\ZZ/F_{m+2}\ZZ\),
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  \sum_{r=0}^{F_{m+2}-1} d_m^\#(r)^q
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1} d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))^q.
\]
Now apply \Cref{thm:partition-difference}.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Fibonacci-window sandwich]
\label{thm:fibonacci-window-sandwich}
For every pair of integers \(q\ge 1\) and \(m\ge 1\),
\[
  T_q^\dagger(F_{m+1})\le S_q(m)\le T_q^\dagger(F_{m+2}).
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By \Cref{thm:difference-power-sums}, for \(0\le n<F_{m+1}\) we have
\(R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})=0\), hence \(a_m(n)=R^\dagger(n)\).  Therefore
\[
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+1}-1} a_m(n)^q
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+1}-1} R^\dagger(n)^q
  =
  T_q^\dagger(F_{m+1}),
\]
which gives the lower bound.

For the upper bound, \(a_m(n)\) is a fiber multiplicity and hence nonnegative.
By definition,
\[
  a_m(n)=R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\le R^\dagger(n)
  \qquad (0\le n<F_{m+2}).
\]
Raise both sides to the \(q\)-th power and sum over \(n\).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Transfer of partition constants]
\label{thm:all-q-transfer}
For every integer \(q\ge 1\),
\[
  T_q^\dagger(N)\asymp_q N^{(\log \lambda_q)/\log\varphi},
\]
where \(\lambda_q\) is Sanna's partition constant \cite[Thm.~1.1]{Sanna2025}.
More explicitly, Sanna's theorem is applied to
\[
  T_q(N):=\sum_{0\le n<N}R(n)^q
\]
with the convention that \(R(n)\) counts partitions into Fibonacci parts
\(F_2,F_3,\dots\), so the two unit weights are not distinguished; in that
normalization \(T_q(N)\asymp_q
N^{(\log\lambda_q)/\log\varphi}\).  Hence
\[
  S_q(m)\asymp_q \lambda_q^m.
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
We first align the external convention with the indexed model of this paper.
Our \(R(n)\) is the same unoriented Fibonacci partition function as in
Sanna's theorem: the part \(1\) occurs once, namely as \(F_2\), and
\(R(n)=0\) for \(n<0\).  The indexed function
\(R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\) merely records whether the missing \(F_1=1\) unit
has been used, and therefore changes power sums only by constants depending on
\(q\).
Pointwise,
\[
  R(n)\le R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\le 2\max\{R(n),R(n-1)\},
\]
so
\[
  R(n)^q\le R^\dagger(n)^q
  \le 2^{q-1}\bigl(R(n)^q+R(n-1)^q\bigr).
\]
Summing over \(0\le n<N\) yields
\[
  \sum_{n=0}^{N-1} R(n)^q
  \le
  T_q^\dagger(N)
  \le
  2^q\sum_{n=0}^{N-1} R(n)^q.
\]
Here the last inequality uses \(R(-1)=0\), so
\[
  \sum_{n=0}^{N-1}R(n-1)^q
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{N-2}R(n)^q
  \le
  \sum_{n=0}^{N-1}R(n)^q.
\]
Sanna's theorem \cite[Thm.~1.1]{Sanna2025} therefore gives
\[
  T_q^\dagger(N)\asymp_q N^{(\log \lambda_q)/\log\varphi}.
\]
Taking \(N=F_{m+1}\) and \(N=F_{m+2}\), using \(F_m\asymp\varphi^m\), and
applying \Cref{thm:fibonacci-window-sandwich}, we obtain
\[
  S_q(m)\asymp_q \lambda_q^m.
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[The case \texorpdfstring{$q=1$}{q=1}]
\label{rem:lambda-one}
For \(q=1\), \(S_1(m)=2^m\) gives \(\lambda_1=2\) directly.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Relation to classical moment problems]
\label{rem:sanna-relation}
Theorem~\ref{thm:difference-power-sums} places the finite-window collision
moments beside the ordinary power sums of the Fibonacci partition function
studied by Sanna \cite{Sanna2025}.  Theorem~\ref{thm:fibonacci-window-sandwich}
sharpens that relation: every finite-window collision moment is exactly trapped
between two adjacent Fibonacci windows of the indexed partition power sums.
Theorem~\ref{thm:all-q-transfer} then shows that the fold moments share the
same exponential constants \(\lambda_q\).
\end{remark}

\begin{corollary}[Constant-term formula]
\label{cor:multivariate-moment-kernel}
Fix \(q\ge 2\), write \(\mathbf z=(z_1,\dots,z_{q-1})\), and for
\(A\subseteq [q-1]\) write
\[
  z_A:=\prod_{i\in A}z_i,
  \qquad
  z_\varnothing:=1.
\]
Then
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  [\mathbf z^0]
  \prod_{j=1}^m
  \left(
    2+\sum_{\varnothing\neq A\subseteq [q-1]}
    \bigl(z_A^{F_j}+z_A^{-F_j}\bigr)
  \right).
\]
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
By \Cref{thm:affine-transfer},
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}\widetilde d_m(n)^q.
\]
Let
\[
  \mathcal P_m(z):=\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}\widetilde d_m(n)z^n.
\]
Diagonal extraction gives
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  [\mathbf z^0]\,
  \mathcal P_m(z_1)\cdots \mathcal P_m(z_{q-1})
  \mathcal P_m\bigl((z_1\cdots z_{q-1})^{-1}\bigr).
\]
Using the product form of \(\mathcal P_m\), we obtain
\[
  S_q(m)
  =
  [\mathbf z^0]
  \prod_{j=1}^m
  \left(
    \prod_{i=1}^{q-1}(1+z_i^{F_j})
  \right)
  \left(
    1+(z_1\cdots z_{q-1})^{-F_j}
  \right).
\]
Finally,
\begin{align*}
  \prod_{i=1}^{q-1}(1+z_i)\left(1+(z_1\cdots z_{q-1})^{-1}\right)
  &=
  \sum_{A\subseteq [q-1]}z_A
  +
  \sum_{A\subseteq [q-1]}z_{A^c}^{-1} \\
  &=
  2+\sum_{\varnothing\neq A\subseteq [q-1]}(z_A+z_A^{-1}),
\end{align*}
and replacing \(z_i\) by \(z_i^{F_j}\) yields the claim.
\end{proof}

\subsection{The quadratic case}

\begin{definition}[Quadratic difference energy]
\label{def:T2}
Set
\[
  T_2(m):=S_2(m)=\sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1} a_m(n)^2.
\]
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Cubic recurrence]
\label{thm:cubic-recurrence}
The quadratic difference energy satisfies
\[
  T_2(1)=2,\qquad T_2(2)=6,\qquad T_2(3)=14,
\]
and for every \(m\ge 4\),
\[
  T_2(m)=2T_2(m-1)+2T_2(m-2)-2T_2(m-3).
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By \Cref{thm:partition-difference},
\[
  T_2(m)
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))^2
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}\widetilde d_m(n)^2.
\]
Let
\[
  \mathcal P_m(z):=\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})
  =
  \sum_{n=0}^{F_{m+2}-1}\widetilde d_m(n)z^n.
\]
Then
\[
  T_2(m)
  =
  [z^0]\bigl(\mathcal P_m(z)\mathcal P_m(z^{-1})\bigr).
\]
Define
\[
  B_m(h):=[z^h]\prod_{j=1}^m\bigl(2+z^{F_j}+z^{-F_j}\bigr),
  \qquad h\in\ZZ.
\]
Then \(B_m(h)=B_m(-h)\) and \(T_2(m)=B_m(0)\).

The defining product yields the coefficient recursion
\begin{equation}\label{eq:Bm-rec}
  B_m(h)=2B_{m-1}(h)+B_{m-1}(h-F_m)+B_{m-1}(h+F_m).
\end{equation}
Taking \(h=0\) and using symmetry gives
\begin{equation}\label{eq:T-U}
  T_2(m)=2T_2(m-1)+2B_{m-1}(F_m).
\end{equation}
Introduce the auxiliary quantity
\[
  U_m:=B_m(F_{m+1}).
\]
Then \eqref{eq:T-U} becomes
\begin{equation}\label{eq:T-U2}
  T_2(m)=2T_2(m-1)+2U_{m-1}.
\end{equation}

Apply \eqref{eq:Bm-rec} with \(h=F_{m+1}\):
\[
  U_m
  =
  2B_{m-1}(F_{m+1})
  +B_{m-1}(F_{m+1}-F_m)
  +B_{m-1}(F_{m+1}+F_m).
\]
The support of \(\prod_{j=1}^{m-1}(2+z^{F_j}+z^{-F_j})\) lies in
\[
  |h|\le \sum_{j=1}^{m-1}F_j=F_{m+1}-1,
\]
so
\[
  B_{m-1}(F_{m+1})=0
  \qquad\text{and}\qquad
  B_{m-1}(F_{m+1}+F_m)=0.
\]
Since \(F_{m+1}-F_m=F_{m-1}\), we obtain
\begin{equation}\label{eq:U-shift}
  U_m=B_{m-1}(F_{m-1}).
\end{equation}

Now apply \eqref{eq:Bm-rec} with index \(m-1\) and \(h=F_{m-1}\):
\[
  B_{m-1}(F_{m-1})
  =
  2B_{m-2}(F_{m-1})
  +B_{m-2}(0)
  +B_{m-2}(2F_{m-1}).
\]
Again the support bound
\[
  \sum_{j=1}^{m-2}F_j=F_m-1<2F_{m-1}
\]
forces \(B_{m-2}(2F_{m-1})=0\).  Therefore
\begin{equation}\label{eq:U-rec}
  U_m=2U_{m-2}+T_2(m-2).
\end{equation}
Replacing \(m\) by \(m-1\) in \eqref{eq:U-rec} gives
\[
  U_{m-1}=2U_{m-3}+T_2(m-3).
\]
From \eqref{eq:T-U2} with index \(m-2\),
\[
  T_2(m-2)=2T_2(m-3)+2U_{m-3},
\]
so
\begin{equation}\label{eq:U-elim}
  2U_{m-1}=2T_2(m-2)-2T_2(m-3).
\end{equation}
Substituting \eqref{eq:U-elim} into \eqref{eq:T-U2} yields
\[
  T_2(m)=2T_2(m-1)+2T_2(m-2)-2T_2(m-3).
\]
The initial values are obtained directly from the cases \(m=1,2,3\).
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Generating function]
\label{cor:sigma-generating}
The sequence \((T_2(m))_{m\ge 1}\) has generating function
\[
  \sum_{m\ge 1}T_2(m)u^m
  =
  \frac{2u+2u^2-2u^3}{1-2u-2u^2+2u^3}.
\]
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Apply the standard generating-function calculation to the recurrence of
\Cref{thm:cubic-recurrence}.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Alternating second-order term]
\label{thm:q2-alternating-second-order}
Let
\[
  p(\lambda):=\lambda^3-2\lambda^2-2\lambda+2.
\]
Then \(p\) has exactly three distinct real roots
\[
  \lambda_-\in(-2,-1),
  \qquad
  \lambda_+\in(0,1),
  \qquad
  \rho\in(2,3).
\]
Consequently there exist real constants \(c_\rho,c_-,c_+\) such that
\[
  T_2(m)=c_\rho\rho^m+c_-\lambda_-^m+c_+\lambda_+^m
  \qquad (m\ge 1),
\]
with \(c_\rho>0\) and \(c_-\neq 0\).  In particular,
\[
  T_2(m)-c_\rho\rho^m
  =
  c_-\lambda_-^m+O(\lambda_+^m),
\]
so the second-order term is eventually alternating in sign.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The sign checks
\[
  p(-2)=-10,\qquad p(-1)=1,\qquad p(0)=2,
\]
\[
  p(1)=-1,\qquad p(2)=-2,\qquad p(3)=5
\]
show that \(p\) has roots in the three disjoint intervals
\((-2,-1)\), \((0,1)\), and \((2,3)\).  Since \(p\) is cubic, these are all
its roots, and they are distinct.

By \Cref{thm:cubic-recurrence}, \((T_2(m))\) is a linear recurrence with
characteristic polynomial \(p\).  Since the roots are simple, there exist
constants \(c_\rho,c_-,c_+\in\RR\) such that
\[
  T_2(m)=c_\rho\rho^m+c_-\lambda_-^m+c_+\lambda_+^m.
\]
Because \(T_2(m)>0\) and \(\rho\) is the unique dominant root, one has
\(c_\rho>0\).

It remains to show \(c_-\neq 0\).  Suppose instead that \(c_-=0\).  Then
\((T_2(m))\) would satisfy the second-order recurrence with roots \(\rho\) and
\(\lambda_+\):
\[
  T_2(m+2)=(\rho+\lambda_+)T_2(m+1)-\rho\lambda_+T_2(m).
\]
Applying this at \(m=1\) and using \((T_2(1),T_2(2),T_2(3))=(2,6,14)\) yields
\[
  14=6(\rho+\lambda_+)-2\rho\lambda_+.
\]
By Vieta's formulas for \(p\),
\[
  \rho+\lambda_+=2-\lambda_-
  \qquad\text{and}\qquad
  \rho\lambda_+=\frac{-2}{\lambda_-}.
\]
Substituting these identities gives
\[
  14=12-6\lambda_-+\frac{4}{\lambda_-},
\]
so \(\lambda_-\) would satisfy
\[
  3\lambda_-^2+\lambda_--2=0.
\]
Dividing \(p(\lambda)\) by \(3\lambda^2+\lambda-2\) gives remainder
\((4-5\lambda)/9\), so any common root would satisfy \(\lambda=4/5\), which is
impossible because \(\lambda_-\in(-2,-1)\).  This contradiction proves
\(c_-\neq 0\).  Since \(|\lambda_-|>\lambda_+\) and \(\lambda_-<0\), the
second-order term is eventually alternating.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Chow--Jones]
\label{rem:chow-jones}
Chow and Jones \cite{ChowJones2024} showed that the variance of the Fibonacci
partition function is governed by the same cubic
\(\lambda^3-2\lambda^2-2\lambda+2\).  Theorem~\ref{thm:cubic-recurrence} gives
a finite-window explanation: it controls the quadratic energy of the
Fibonacci-lag discrete derivative \(a_m(n)\).  Since
\Cref{thm:all-q-transfer} yields \(T_2(m)\asymp \lambda_2^m\), its dominant
root \(\rho\) is exactly \(\lambda_2\).
\end{remark}


 succeeded in 379ms:
\section{Integer pressure and a diagonal high-tilt limit}
\label{sec:integer-pressure}

For notational completeness, set
\[
 S_0(m):=|X_m|=F_{m+2},\qquad
 P_0:=\log\varphi,
\]
and, for each positive integer \(q\), set
\[
 P_q:=\lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_q(m)=\log\lambda_q.
\]
The existence of the latter limit follows from
\Cref{thm:all-q-transfer}.  Thus \(q\) is an integer moment order in this
section; real tilts continue to be denoted by \(t\).

\begin{proposition}[Convexity of the integer pressure]
\label{prop:integer-pressure-convexity}
For every integer \(q\ge1\),
\[
  2P_q\le P_{q-1}+P_{q+1}.
\]
Equivalently, the adjacent differences \(P_q-P_{q-1}\) are nondecreasing.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
For each fixed \(m\), Cauchy--Schwarz gives
\[
 \left(\sum_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)^q\right)^2
 \le
 \left(\sum_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)^{q-1}\right)
 \left(\sum_{x\in X_m}d_m(x)^{q+1}\right).
\]
This is \(S_q(m)^2\le S_{q-1}(m)S_{q+1}(m)\).  Taking logarithms,
dividing by \(m\), and passing to the established limits proves the claim.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Sanna's endpoint law: an alternative finite-window proof]
\label{cor:renyi-endpoint}
One has
\[
  \lim_{q\to\infty}\lambda_q^{1/q}=\varphi^{1/2}.
\]
This result is due to Sanna \cite[Thm.~1.2]{Sanna2025}; the proof below is an
alternative deduction from the exact finite-window maximum.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
By \Cref{thm:unconditional-extremal-fibers},
\[
 \lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log D_m=\frac12\log\varphi.
\]
For every fixed positive integer \(q\),
\[
 D_m^q\le S_q(m)\le F_{m+2}D_m^q.
\]
After taking logarithms, dividing by \(m\), and letting \(m\to\infty\),
\Cref{thm:all-q-transfer} gives
\[
 \frac q2\log\varphi
 \le \log\lambda_q
 \le \log\varphi+\frac q2\log\varphi.
\]
Division by \(q\), followed by \(q\to\infty\), proves the displayed limit.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Diagonal high-tilt limit]
\label{thm:diagonal-high-moments}
For every sequence of positive integers \(q_m\to\infty\),
\[
 \frac{1}{m q_m}\log S_{q_m}(m)
 \longrightarrow\frac12\log\varphi.
\]
More generally, for every real sequence \(t_m\ge1\) with
\(t_m\to\infty\),
\[
 \frac{1}{m t_m}\log S_{t_m}(m)
 \longrightarrow\frac12\log\varphi.
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For every real \(t\ge1\),
\[
 D_m^t\le S_t(m)\le |X_m|D_m^t=F_{m+2}D_m^t.
\]
Putting \(t=t_m\), taking logarithms, and dividing by \(mt_m\) gives
\[
 \frac1m\log D_m
 \le \frac{1}{mt_m}\log S_{t_m}(m)
 \le \frac1m\log D_m+\frac{\log F_{m+2}}{mt_m}.
\]
The first term tends to \(\frac12\log\varphi\), and the last term tends to
zero because \(\log F_{m+2}=O(m)\) and \(t_m\to\infty\).  The integer
statement is the specialization \(t_m=q_m\).
\end{proof}

import mpmath as mp
mp.mp.dps=50
z=lambda s: mp.zeta(s-1)/mp.zeta(s)
sigma=mp.findroot(lambda s:z(s)-2,(2.4,2.6))
kappa=-mp.diff(z,sigma)
phi=(1+mp.sqrt(5))/2
beta=mp.log(phi)/mp.log(2/phi)
rho=mp.findroot(lambda x:x**3-2*x**2-2*x+2,2.5)
vals={
'sigma0':sigma,'kappa':kappa,'beta':beta,'width':sigma-beta,'rho':rho,
'Hinf':mp.log(2/mp.sqrt(phi)),'H2':mp.log(4/rho),'H0':mp.log(phi),
'chi':mp.log(phi*rho/4)
}
for k,v in vals.items(): print(k, mp.nstr(v,30))
print('oracle kappa error', mp.mpf('2.58918437999')-kappa)
print('oracle beta error', mp.mpf('2.27055945396')-beta)

---
name: "pdf"
description: "Use when tasks involve reading, creating, or reviewing PDF files where rendering and layout matter; prefer visual checks by rendering pages (Poppler) and use Python tools such as `reportlab`, `pdfplumber`, and `pypdf` for generation and extraction."
---


# PDF Skill

## When to use
- Read or review PDF content where layout and visuals matter.
- Create PDFs programmatically with reliable formatting.
- Validate final rendering before delivery.

## Workflow
1. Prefer visual review: render PDF pages to PNGs and inspect them.
   - Use `pdftoppm` if available.
   - If unavailable, install Poppler or ask the user to review the output locally.
2. Use `reportlab` to generate PDFs when creating new documents.
3. Use `pdfplumber` (or `pypdf`) for text extraction and quick checks; do not rely on it for layout fidelity.
4. After each meaningful update, re-render pages and verify alignment, spacing, and legibility.

## Temp and output conventions
- Use `tmp/pdfs/` for intermediate files; delete when done.
- Write final artifacts under `output/pdf/` when working in this repo.
- Keep filenames stable and descriptive.

## Dependencies (install if missing)
Prefer `uv` for dependency management.

Python packages:
```
uv pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```
If `uv` is unavailable:
```
python3 -m pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```
System tools (for rendering):
```
# macOS (Homebrew)
brew install poppler

# Ubuntu/Debian
sudo apt-get install -y poppler-utils
```

If installation isn't possible in this environment, tell the user which dependency is missing and how to install it locally.

## Environment
No required environment variables.

## Rendering command
```
pdftoppm -png $INPUT_PDF $OUTPUT_PREFIX
```

## Quality expectations
- Maintain polished visual design: consistent typography, spacing, margins, and section hierarchy.
- Avoid rendering issues: clipped text, overlapping elements, broken tables, black squares, or unreadable glyphs.
- Charts, tables, and images must be sharp, aligned, and clearly labeled.
- Use ASCII hyphens only. Avoid U+2011 (non-breaking hyphen) and other Unicode dashes.
- Citations and references must be human-readable; never leave tool tokens or placeholder strings.

## Final checks
- Do not deliver until the latest PNG inspection shows zero visual or formatting defects.
- Confirm headers/footers, page numbering, and section transitions look polished.
- Keep intermediate files organized or remove them after final approval.

# G. Circle Dimension and Haar Pullback

## 工作定位

- 目标题目：`Circle Dimension, Haar Pullback, and Poisson--Cauchy Gram Kernels`
- 目标期刊：
  - 首选：`Journal of Functional Analysis`
  - 次选：`Annales de l'Institut Fourier`
- 本稿定位：
  - 只保留一条主线：`Cayley 紧化 -> Haar 回拉唯一性 -> circle dimension -> Poisson--Cauchy / Gram kernel`
  - 不引入与本线无直接关系的 Prym / Hurwitz / Nielsen / Langlands 分支

## 本稿当前应保留的核心定理包

### 代数主线

- Haar 回拉唯一性与规范重参数刚性
- circle dimension 的公理刚性
- 短正合列加法、rank-nullity、`Hom / Ext / tensor` 算律
- phase spectrum
  - 极限刻画 `\cdim`
  - 从 `N -> |\Hom(G,\mu_N)|` 完整重构 `G`
  - 相位谱像的内禀刻画
- half-circle dimension 的 dyadic prefix 操作型解释
- 二阶异常律与最小 injectivization 成本

### 分析主线

- precision potential 与 entropy ledger
- Poisson 半群与 relative-entropy contraction
- 高阶 Fisher 能量闭式
- 二阶正规形、锐 `L^p` 渐近、KL 主项
- KL 六阶校正与归一化 KL 的最终单调性
- phase Fourier 有限模态与 `(\sigma^2,\mu_3,\mu_4)` 反演
- central slice 的 Stieltjes 可识别性
- central two-channel 对全分布的唯一重构
- Cauchy 导数 Gram 闭式与差核 `2 / (4 + (a-b)^2)`
- 差核 RKHS 的仿射复合、条带延拓、整数采样稳定性

## 本次从主论文抽取到的“更深定理”

源材料：

- `..\2026_golden_ratio_driven_scan_projection_generation_recursive_emergence`
- 重点来源：
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/subsubsec__circle-dimension-phase-gate-cdim-fg-abelian-calculus.tex`
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/subsec__circle-dimension-phase-gate-operational-half-circle-dimension.tex`
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/subsec__circle-dimension-phase-gate-poisson-cauchy-constants.tex`
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/para__circle-dimension-phase-gate-poisson-cauchy-phase-fourierization.tex`
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/subsec__circle-dimension-phase-gate-poisson-cauchy-gram-kernel.tex`
  - `sections/body/circle_dimension_phase_gate/subsec__circle-dimension-phase-gate-cauchy-kernel-rkhs-affine-translate.tex`

本稿已优先吸收以下高价值结果：

- `circle dimension` 的公理刚性、phase spectrum 重构与可实现性判据
- `\NN^d` 在 dyadic prefix 下的操作型 half-circle law
- 高阶 Fisher 能量
- moment-matching 下的 KL 主项思想
- phase-mode 矩反演
- central slice / two-channel reconstruction
- Gram closed form 与差核 RKHS

## 对主论文的补全/修正建议

按你的要求，这些建议只记录在本稿 README，不直接改主论文。

### 数学结构层面

- 主论文里的 `circle_dimension_phase_gate` 已经包含一组足以独立成文的 JFA 级主结果，但目前分散在多个子文件里，主线不够显式。
- 建议主论文后续把下面几条结论提到更前面的位置，并作为一个可单独引用的 theorem package：
  - `cdim` 公理刚性
  - phase spectrum reconstruction
  - phase spectrum characterization
  - higher-order Fisher energies
  - phase-mode inversion
  - central two-channel reconstruction
  - Gram kernel / RKHS package
- KL 六阶校正、最终单调性、two-divergence moment tomography 这些结果在主论文里是“高价值但埋得过深”的结论，后续应当明确标成一组 asymptotic package，而不是只作为长节中的局部命题存在。

### 写作组织层面

- 主论文把“基础接口定理”和“大量旁支应用”混在一个 section 中，导致可移植性差。
- 建议把主论文后续整理成：
  - interface theorems
  - asymptotic theorems
  - reconstruction theorems
  - RKHS / operator-theoretic consequences
  - side branches / arithmetic applications

### 工程层面

- 本稿编译时暴露出一个重要问题：`sec_precision_potential.tex` 曾经以隐式方式被 TeX 搜索路径解析，而不是作为本目录中的显式源文件存在。
- 这说明相关分析结果在工程组织上缺少“可移植的局部封装”。
- 对主论文的建议是：任何准备外拆成 publication 版本的 section，都必须先保证：
  - 本地显式文件存在
  - 不依赖未声明的 TeX 搜索路径命中
  - theorem label 与 bibliography key 可独立解析

## 当前本稿的编辑原则

- 不再从主论文机械复制长段文本
- 只抽取可直接服务于 JFA 主线的定理
- 所有迁入结果都要重写成更紧凑的英文论文表达
- 优先保证：
  - theorem chain 清晰
  - proofs 可读
  - bibliography 可编译
  - section 之间没有隐式依赖

"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "@'
import math
from collections import Counter
import mpmath as mp

M=28
F=[0]*(M+6); F[1]=F[2]=1
for i in range(3,len(F)): F[i]=F[i-1]+F[i-2]
# ordinary R: one each of F_2,..., enough for the largest two-layer endpoint
maxn=F[M+3]-2
R=[0]*(maxn+1); R[0]=1
for j in range(2,M+3):
    w=F[j]
    for n in range(maxn,w-1,-1): R[n]+=R[n-w]

def layer(j):
    return R[F[j+1]-1:F[j+2]-1]
N={(j,k):c for j in range(M+2) for k,c in Counter(layer(j)).items()}
# free-monoid Dirichlet coefficients Psi
NMAX=max(max(layer(j)) for j in range(M+1))
phi=list(range(NMAX+1))
for p in range(2,NMAX+1):
    if phi[p]==p:
        for q in range(p,NMAX+1,p): phi[q]-=phi[q]//p
psi=[0]*(NMAX+1); psi[1]=1
for q in range(1,NMAX+1):
    pq=psi[q]
    if pq:
        for d in range(2,NMAX//q+1): psi[q*d]+=pq*phi[d]

fail=[]; one_rows=[]; max_bound_ratio=0
critical=[]; entropy=[]
coeff=[1]
mp.mp.dps=40
sig=mp.findroot(lambda s:mp.zeta(s-1)/mp.zeta(s)-2,2.48)
kappa=-mp.diff(lambda s:mp.zeta(s-1)/mp.zeta(s),sig)
phi_g=(1+math.sqrt(5))/2
rho=float(mp.findroot(lambda x:x**3-2*x**2-2*x+2,2.48))
for m in range(1,M+1):
    w=F[m]
    coeff += [0]*w
    for n in range(len(coeff)-1,w-1,-1): coeff[n]+=coeff[n-w]
    C=Counter(coeff)
    # two-layer identity
    C2=Counter(layer(m)); C2.update(layer(m+1))
    if C"'!=C2: fail.append(('"'two-layer',m))
    ones=[i for i,v in enumerate(coeff) if v==1]
    if len(ones)"'!=2: fail.append(('"'ones',m,len(ones),ones))
    one_rows.append((m,ones,[F[m+1]-1,F[m+2]-1]))
    for k,c in C.items():
        if k>=2:
            if c>4*psi[k]: fail.append(('uniform',m,k,c,psi[k]))
            max_bound_ratio=max(max_bound_ratio,c/(4*psi[k]))
            if m>=2*k and c"'!=4*psi[k]: fail.append(('"'stable',m,k,c,psi[k]))
    Scrit=sum(float(v)**(-float(sig)) for v in coeff)
    critical.append((m,Scrit))
    H0=math.log(len(coeff))/m
    H2=(2*m*math.log(2)-math.log(sum(v*v for v in coeff)))/m
    Hinf=(m*math.log(2)-math.log(max(coeff)))/m
    chi=(math.log(len(coeff))+math.log(sum(v*v for v in coeff))-m*math.log(4))/m
    entropy.append((m,H0,H2,Hinf,chi,max(coeff)))

print('finite_identity_failures=',fail[:10], 'count=',len(fail))
print('max_uniform_bound_ratio=',max_bound_ratio)
print('ones_first_last=',one_rows[:3],one_rows[-1])
for s in (2.6,3.0,4.0):
    Z=float(4/(2-mp.zeta(s-1)/mp.zeta(s))-2)
    rows=[]
    coeff=[1]
    for m in range(1,M+1):
        w=F[m]; coeff += [0]*w
        for n in range(len(coeff)-1,w-1,-1): coeff[n]+=coeff[n-w]
        if m in (10,16,22,28): rows.append((m,sum(v**(-s) for v in coeff),Z))
    print('partition_limit_s=',s,'rows=',rows)
print('critical_S selected=',[critical[i-1] for i in (8,12,16,20,24,28)])
print('critical_lower_ratio S/logm=',[(m,v/math.log(m)) for m,v in critical if m in (12,16,20,24,28)],'target=',4/float(kappa))
print('entropy selected=',[entropy[i-1] for i in (8,12,16,20,24,28)])
print('entropy_targets=',math.log(phi_g),math.log(4/rho),math.log(2/math.sqrt(phi_g)),math.log(phi_g*rho/4))
# Gibbs total variation to exact limiting law, truncating limit at computed max k and adding exact tail bound numerically from psi up to NMAX
for s in (2.6,3.0,4.0):
    Z=float(4/(2-mp.zeta(s-1)/mp.zeta(s))-2)
    coeff=[1]
    for m in range(1,M+1):
        w=F[m]; coeff += [0]*w
        for n in range(len(coeff)-1,w-1,-1): coeff[n]+=coeff[n-w]
        if m in (12,20,28):
            C=Counter(coeff); Sm=sum(v**(-s) for v in coeff)
            upto=max(max(C),NMAX)
            l1=abs(C.get(1,0)/Sm-2/Z)
            for k in range(2,upto+1): l1+=abs(C.get(k,0)*k**(-s)/Sm-4*psi[k]*k**(-s)/Z)
            # omitted exact limiting tail is nonnegative; approximate via Z minus known partial mass
            known=(2+sum(4*psi[k]*k**(-s) for k in range(2,NMAX+1)))/Z
            l1+=max(0.0,1-known)
            print('TV',s,m,0.5*l1)
# Tauber coefficients to 200000 independent of layer data
X=200000
ph=list(range(X+1))
for p in range(2,X+1):
    if ph[p]==p:
        for q in range(p,X+1,p): ph[q]-=ph[q]//p
ps=[0]*(X+1); ps[1]=1
for q in range(1,X+1):
    if ps[q]:
        for d in range(2,X//q+1): ps[q*d]+=ps[q]*ph[d]
cs=0; ws=0.0
for k in range(2,X+1):
    cs+=ps[k]; ws+=ps[k]*k**(-float(sig))
    if k in (1000,10000,100000,200000):
        main=k**float(sig)/(float(sig)*float(kappa))
        print('tauber',k,'count_ratio',cs/main,'weighted/log_ratio',ws/(math.log(k)/float(kappa)))

codex
The finite computation over every window \(1\le m\le28\) found no internal counterexample to the Zeckendorf identities: the two-layer spectrum, the uniform \(4\Psi(k)\) bound, the \(m\ge2k\) stabilization, the negative-temperature limits, and the four entropy-rate constants all behaved as claimed. The Tauberian coefficient test up to \(k=200{,}000\) gave normalized ratios \(1.00059\) for the counting asymptotic and \(0.96287\) for the logarithmic asymptotic. These checks support the mathematics only in the unrelated sibling model; they do not repair the absence of that model from this manuscript.

# Literature Check: Regularly Varying Poisson-Entropy Boundary Layer

Checked 2026-08-02 (Asia/Singapore). The search target was the
coefficient-subtracted large-scale asymptotic

\[
t^{2N}\left\{
D_{\mathrm{KL}}(P_t*\nu\Vert P_t)
-\sum_{j=2}^{N-1}A_{2j}(\nu)t^{-2j}
\right\}
\]

for a fixed centred law whose tail is regularly varying with the exact
missing-moment index \(2N-2\).

## arXiv API Queries

The following requests were made directly to the required HTTP API endpoint.
The returned Atom feeds were inspected through their
`opensearch:totalResults` fields and, for nonempty feeds, through their
entry titles and abstracts.

| `http://export.arxiv.org/api/query?search_query=all:%22Cauchy%20semigroup%22%20AND%20all:entropy%20AND%20all:asymptotic&start=0&max_results=25` | 0 | No direct match. |

## Standard External Inputs

The new proof invokes only the regular-variation results in the first item
below. The remaining items are exact contextual comparators; their theorems
are not imported into the boundary-layer proof.

- N. H. Bingham, C. M. Goldie, and J. L. Teugels,
  *Regular Variation*, Cambridge University Press, 1987,
  DOI 10.1017/CBO9780511721434. Proposition 1.5.9a supplies the divergent
  integrated-slow-variation fact
  \(L(t)=o(\int^tL(s)\,ds/s)\); Theorem 1.5.11 supplies Karamata's
  direct and tail integral theorems. This is the only external theorem used
  in the new proof.
- Laurens de Haan and Ana Ferreira, *Extreme Value Theory: An
  Introduction*, Springer, 2006, DOI 10.1007/0-387-34471-3. This records the
  de Haan/second-order regular-variation framework. No second-order theorem
  from this source is used; the paper explicitly leaves second-order entropy
  corrections open.
- N. G. de Bruijn, *A property of heat equation connected with Shannon's
  theory of information*, Nederl. Akad. Wetensch. Proc. Ser. A 56 (1953),
  80-81. No DOI or arXiv identifier was found. This is historical context,
  not a proof input.
- The Hardy--Stein and stable-entropy papers listed above are contextual
  comparators, not proof inputs.

DOI metadata for the books and journal articles was independently checked
against the Crossref REST endpoint
`https://api.crossref.org/works/<DOI>`.

## Novelty Determination

No arXiv record located by the targeted or comparator queries states the
following combination: a fixed centred input law, harmonic Poisson/Cauchy
smoothing, subtraction of all finite Cayley--entropy coefficients below the
missing moment, a tail index exactly \(2N-2\), and the explicit limit
normalized by
\(\ell_L(t)=\int^tL(s)\,ds/s\).

The nearest results separate into three established bodies of work:
Karamata/de Haan regular-variation theory; de Bruijn or Hardy--Stein entropy
dissipation identities; and entropic convergence to stable laws. The
manuscript cites those inputs and does not claim any of their theorems as
new. Its new statement is the quotient-to-entropy synthesis yielding the
constant
\[
p(c_++c_-)(-1)^N(N-1)2^{-2N+2}\mu_2.
\]

This is a documented negative literature search, not a logically exhaustive
proof that no publication exists outside the indexed sources. Within the
queried arXiv corpus and the standard references/comparators above, no prior
publication of the stated theorem was found.

+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。
+
+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+

+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。
+
+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+

Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。

@@ -0,0 +1,21 @@
+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。
+
+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

@@ -0,0 +1,21 @@
+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。
+
+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。

上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。

对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。

对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。

对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。

对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。

对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。

对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。

下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有

lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),

其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。

codex
**(i) Per-Claim Verdicts**

All verdicts below concern the claims as proposed results of this manuscript. The active manuscript [main.tex](D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/main.tex) studies Poisson/Cauchy convolution, Cayley–Chebyshev modes, relative-entropy asymptotics, regularly varying boundary layers, and Bregman dissipation. It contains no definitions of \(X_m,d_m,C_m(k),R(n),\Psi(k),S_t(m),D_m,p_m(t)\), Fibonacci layers, or Zeckendorf fibers. Those definitions and the quoted “two adjacent Fibonacci layers” identity occur instead in the separate sibling manuscript `2026_finite_window_zeckendorf_fibers_discrete_thermodynamics_tams`.

1. **Theorem 1, finite-window microcanonical spectrum: WRONG.**

   The asserted deduction from “本文式 (4.10)” and “本文 uniform layer-count lemma” has a false premise: neither result exists in this Cayley–Poisson manuscript. Thus \(C_m(k)\) is not even defined under the paper’s hypotheses. As a result about the unrelated Zeckendorf model, the reasoning was independently reconstructed: exhaustive subset-sum computation for every \(1\le m\le28\) verified the two-layer identity, the two singleton fibers, \(C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k)\), and \(C_m(k)=4\Psi(k)\) whenever \(m\ge2k\), with no counterexample. That separate verification does not make the statement a theorem of this paper.

2. **Theorem 2, negative-temperature partition-function limit: WRONG.**

   \(S_{-s}(m)\) and the finite-window limit are undefined in this manuscript, and the proof depends entirely on the misplaced Theorem 1. The Dirichlet-series input
   \[
   1+\sum_{k\ge2}\Psi(k)k^{-s}
   =\left(2-\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}\right)^{-1}
   \]
   is already published in F. V. Weinstein, *Notes on Fibonacci Partitions*, *Experimental Mathematics* 25 (2016), Theorem 10.3, DOI `10.1080/10586458.2015.1118416`. In the alien Zeckendorf model, direct computation at \(s=3,4\) converged toward the asserted limits \(4.3334509111\ldots\) and \(2.4975481717\ldots\), respectively; this cannot supply a Cayley–Poisson theorem.

3. **Theorem 3, limiting Gibbs spectrum and critical pole: WRONG.**

   Neither \(Q_{m,s}\), \(Q_s\), nor the random variable \(K\) belongs to the probability spaces defined by this paper. The scalar calculations themselves were checked:
   \[
   \sigma_0=2.4787507857339602607\ldots,\qquad
   \kappa=2.5891843799469241261\ldots,
   \]
   and differentiation of the meromorphic Laurent expansion does yield the stated first-order pole and logarithmic-moment orders in the Zeckendorf setting. Numerically, the total-variation discrepancy at \(s=4\) decreased from \(0.01132\) at \(m=12\) to \(0.000793\) at \(m=28\). None of this connects to the paper’s Poisson/Cauchy entropy functional.

4. **Theorem 4, Tauberian asymptotics for \(\Psi\): WRONG.**

   \(\Psi\) is absent from this manuscript, so this is not a result in its framework. There is also a gap in the proof as written: equality in the positive-coefficient triangle inequality only implies that all phases \(n^{-i\tau}\) are equal; it does not immediately imply \(2^{-i\tau}=3^{-i\tau}=1\). The gap can be closed by first comparing \(n=2\) and \(n=4\), obtaining \(2^{-i\tau}=1\), and then comparing \(n=3\). With that repair, Wiener–Ikehara gives the displayed alien-model asymptotic. An independent coefficient computation through \(k=200{,}000\) produced
   \[
   \frac{\sum_{k\le200000}\Psi(k)}
        {200000^{\sigma_0}/(\sigma_0\kappa)}
   =1.0005865\ldots,
   \]
   and the corresponding logarithmic normalization ratio was \(0.9628718\ldots\). These checks do not cure the cross-paper misattribution.

5. **Theorem 5, compressed zero-pressure interval: WRONG.**

   The active paper defines no finite-window pressure \(p_m(t)\) and has no Fibonacci zero-pressure phase. Its variable \(t\) is the Poisson scale tending to infinity, not a thermodynamic tilt. The internally quoted constants were numerically accurate:
   \[
   \beta_*=2.2705594539596638\ldots,\qquad
   \sigma_0-\beta_*=0.2081913317742965\ldots,
   \]
   and the Jensen and domination inequalities are valid in the separate Zeckendorf model. They have no logical consequence for this manuscript.

6. **Theorem 6, information-loss rates: WRONG.**

   The distribution \(p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m\) and uniform law \(u_m\) do not exist in this manuscript. Moreover, the principal spectral input is not new: the dominant root of
   \[
   x^3-2x^2-2x+2
   \]
   governs the published Fibonacci-partition variance in S. Chow and O. Jones, *On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function*, *Journal of Number Theory* 257 (2024), Theorem 1.1, DOI `10.1016/j.jnt.2023.11.004`. General Fibonacci power-sum growth and its endpoint law are published in C. Sanna, *A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions*, *Discrete Analysis* 2025:2, Theorems 1.1–1.2, arXiv:2309.12724. The quoted numerical rates were independently confirmed:
   \[
   0.452541268030\ldots,\quad
   0.477554342632\ldots,\quad
   0.481211825060\ldots,\quad
   0.003657482428\ldots.
   \]
   Rewriting those unrelated growth constants as Rényi entropies does not produce a theorem about Cayley–Poisson relative entropy.

The arXiv API returned 12 records for `all:"Fibonacci partition"` and zero records for the targeted combinations with `Tauberian`, `Gibbs`, `"negative temperature"`, `"finite-window" AND Zeckendorf`, or `entropy`. Full-text inspection confirmed Weinstein’s stabilization and Dirichlet-series results, Sanna’s power-sum theorems, and the Chow–Jones cubic. This literature audit supports the internal Fibonacci calculations but does not alter the six `WRONG` verdicts caused by the decisive research-object mismatch.

+上一轮答复存在根本性的研究对象错置，六组所谓新定理均须撤回并重新作答。本稿题为《Cayley--Poisson Entropy: Variance Thresholds, Regularly Varying Boundaries, and Coefficient Asymptotics》，研究对象是 Poisson/Cauchy 半群、Cayley--Chebyshev 模态及相对熵渐近。全文没有定义有限 Fibonacci 窗 X_m、折叠纤维重数 d_m、Fibonacci 分拆函数 R(n)、生成轨道数 Psi(k)、碰撞和 S_t(m)、最大纤维 D_m 或压力 p_m(t)。你所援引的“两相邻 Fibonacci 层恒等式”和 uniform layer-count lemma 实际属于另一篇稿件《Finite-Window Zeckendorf Fibers: Affine Partition Correspondence and Negative-Temperature Freezing》，并非本稿。因此，以下反例不是数值偶然，而是对全部六组结论共同前提的直接否定：取本稿任意满足有限均值的概率律 nu，例如 nu=(delta_{-1}+delta_1)/2；本稿由此定义 H(t)=D_KL(P_t*nu || P_t)，但并不产生任何 X_m、d_m 或 C_m(k)，故你陈述的有限窗结论无法由本稿假设形成一个有定义的命题。
+
+对“定理一”，请撤回“由本文式 (4.10)”及“本文 uniform layer-count lemma”所得的论证。本稿不存在所称的纤维--分拆两层接口。即使该结论在另一篇 Zeckendorf 稿件的定义下可由 Weinstein 的单层稳定定理推出，也不得作为本稿的新结果。若重新提出新定理，必须从本稿已经定义的 Cayley 商、Poisson 核、矩条件或 Bregman 耗散出发，并逐项写明假设。
+
+对“定理二”，S_{-s}(m) 在本稿中未定义，且所谓 sigma_0 是 Fibonacci 生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数的收敛横坐标，与本稿的大尺度变量 t 及 KL 渐近没有逻辑联系。该结论依赖错置的定理一，不能移植。Weinstein 已公开给出生成轨道 Dirichlet 级数，若在其他工作中使用，应明确引用 F. V. Weinstein, Notes on Fibonacci Partitions, Experimental Mathematics 25 (2016), 482--499, Theorem 10.3，而不得将该级数重新推导为本稿贡献。
+
+对“定理三”，Q_{m,s}、Q_s 及随机变量 K 均不属于本稿的概率空间。一级极点与 Gibbs 推前的论证即使在 Fibonacci 模型中形式上成立，也不能据此得到本稿的任何 Poisson/Cauchy 熵结论。请勿以符号“熵”或“Gibbs”相同为由混同两个不相关的模型。
+
+对“定理四”，Psi(k) 的 Tauber 型渐近仍然是 Fibonacci 生成轨道问题，而非本稿问题；此外，原证明中“正系数三角不等式取等迫使 2^{-i tau} 与 3^{-i tau} 同时等于 1”的表述缺少一步：取等首先只说明所有相位相同，须再比较 n=2 与 n=4 才能推出 2^{-i tau}=1，继而比较 n=3。即使补足此步，该定理仍不得写入本稿。Weinstein 的 Theorems 5.1 与 10.3 是公开输入，必须引用，不得复述其证明充当新贡献。
+
+对“定理五”，本稿没有有限窗压力 p_m(t)，也没有所谓零压力相变区间。数值区间 [-2.4787507857,-2.2705594540] 属于另一篇 Fibonacci 热力学稿件，不能用于压缩本稿的任何未知区间。请完全停止沿实倾斜 Fibonacci 压力方向展开。
+
+对“定理六”，有限窗输出分布 p_m(x)=d_m(x)/2^m 在本稿中不存在。三次多项式 x^3-2x^2-2x+2 及其主根已经出现在 S. Chow and O. Jones, On the variance of the Fibonacci partition function, Journal of Number Theory 257 (2024), 341--353, Theorem 1.1；Fibonacci 分拆幂矩常数及其端点律见 C. Sanna, A note on the power sums of the number of Fibonacci partitions, Discrete Analysis 2025:2, Theorems 1.1--1.2。将这些增长率代入 Renyi 熵定义所得的等式不是本稿的结果，更不能作为本稿的主要新定理。
+
+下一轮只回答下述一个本稿内部的尖锐开放问题。设 d>=2，nu 是 R^d 上均值为 bar{gamma}、协方差矩阵 Sigma 有限的概率律，但不假设任何 d+1 阶矩。是否总有
+
+lim_{t->infinity} t^4 D_KL(P_t^{(d)}*nu || P_t^{(d)}(.-bar{gamma})) = Q_d(Sigma),
+
+其中 Q_d 为本稿已经确定的显式协方差二次型？请给出完整证明，或构造一个有限协方差反例；若有限协方差并非充分条件，则须给出在 L^2(Omega_d) 或熵拓扑下的锐必要充分尾条件，并证明相应的有限极限、不同极限或发散机制。答案必须形成可独立审查、具有发表价值的定理与证明，不得仅重复本稿现有的 L>=d+1 的 L^infinity 商估计，也不得把形式 Taylor 展开当作积分余项控制。
+
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.
